As convenient as they are, single-node development environments such as your laptop cannot scale to meet these demands.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRay is a unified way to scale Python and AI applications from a laptop to a cluster.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWith Ray, you can seamlessly scale the same code from a laptop to a cluster. Ray is designed to be general-purpose, meaning that it can performantly run any kind of workload. style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<p dir="auto">Ray is a unified framework for scaling AI and Python applications. Ray consists of a core distributed runtime and a set of AI libraries for simplifying ML compute:</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/ray-project/ray/raw/master/doc/source/images/what-is-ray-padded.svg"><img src="https://github.com/ray-project/ray/raw/master/doc/source/images/what-is-ray-padded.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Learn more about <a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-air/getting-started.html" rel="nofollow">Ray AI Libraries</a>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/data/data.html" rel="nofollow">Data</a>: Scalable Datasets for ML</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/train/train.html" rel="nofollow">Train</a>: Distributed Training</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/tune/index.html" rel="nofollow">Tune</a>: Scalable Hyperparameter Tuning</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/rllib/index.html" rel="nofollow">RLlib</a>: Scalable Reinforcement Learning</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/serve/index.html" rel="nofollow">Serve</a>: Scalable and Programmable Serving</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Or more about <a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-core/walkthrough.html" rel="nofollow">Ray Core</a> and its key abstractions:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-core/tasks.html" rel="nofollow">Tasks</a>: Stateless functions executed in the cluster.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-core/actors.html" rel="nofollow">Actors</a>: Stateful worker processes created in the cluster.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-core/objects.html" rel="nofollow">Objects</a>: Immutable values accessible across the cluster.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Learn more about Monitoring and Debugging:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Monitor Ray apps and clusters with the <a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-core/ray-dashboard.html" rel="nofollow">Ray Dashboard</a>.</li>
<li>Debug Ray apps with the <a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-observability/ray-distributed-debugger.html" rel="nofollow">Ray Distributed Debugger</a>.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Ray runs on any machine, cluster, cloud provider, and Kubernetes, and features a growing
<a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-overview/ray-libraries.html" rel="nofollow">ecosystem of community integrations</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Install Ray with: <code>pip install ray</code>. For nightly wheels, see the
<a href="https://docs.ray.io/en/latest/ray-overview/installation.html" rel="nofollow">Installation page</a>.</p>
<a name="user-content-why-ray"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why Ray?</h2><a id="user-content-why-ray" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why Ray?" href="#why-ray"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Today's ML workloads are increasingly compute-intensive. As convenient as they are, single-node development environments such as your laptop cannot scale to meet these demands.</p>
<p dir="auto">Ray is a unified way to scale Python and AI applications from a laptop to a cluster.</p>
<p dir="auto">With Ray, you can seamlessly scale the same code from a laptop to a cluster. Ray is designed to be general-purpose, meaning that it can performantly run any kind of workload. If your application is written in Python, you can scale it with Ray, no other infrastructure required.</p>
<a name="user-content-more-information"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">More Information</h2><a id="user-content-more-information" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: More Information" href="#more-information"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
